const { useState, useEffect } = React;

// Standalone catalog for peer voting. FilterPanel's COMPOUND_INFO is a label
// and description lookup for two different consumers: study tags (the filter
// UI resolves a tag it found in studies.json through it) and CompoundMap's
// interaction graph (where a missing entry is a hard crash, since compound
// ids are sorted by their label). An entry there does NOT create a filter
// chip -- chips come from real tag counts -- so a compound can legitimately
// live in COMPOUND_INFO with zero tagged studies, as the FGFR3 agents
// currently do. Voting likewise doesn't need a study to exist, so this
// catalog covers every commonly-referenced compound in the space rather than
// only the subset with library coverage.
export const VOTE_CATEGORIES = [

  { id: "skin", label: "Skin" },
  { id: "hair", label: "Hair" },
  { id: "libido", label: "Libido" },
  { id: "sideeffects", label: "Side Effects" },
];

  { id: "lby-135-compound", group: "ancillary", label: "LBY-135", desc: "LBY-135 is a phase 1 research agent catalogued by ChEMBL as an agonist of death receptor 5 (TNFRSF10B). The pathway link describes that curated mechanism, not established safety or clinical benefit." },

function emptyVote() {
  return { up: 0, down: 0, myVote: 0 };
}

function normalizeCompoundName(value) {
  return String(value || "").normalize("NFKD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "");
}

export function CompoundsTab({ user, initialCompoundId = null, onInitialCompoundHandled }) {
  const [votes, setVotes] = useState({});
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [busyKey, setBusyKey] = useState(null);
  const [activeGroup, setActiveGroup] = useState("all");
  const [openCompound, setOpenCompound] = useState(null);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [meta, setMeta] = useState({});
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState("name");
  const [showRequest, setShowRequest] = useState(false);
  const [requestForm, setRequestForm] = useState({ name: "", aliases: "", rationale: "", evidenceUrls: "", safetyNotes: "" });
  const [requestState, setRequestState] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!initialCompoundId) return;
    const compound = COMPOUND_CATALOG.find((item) => item.id === initialCompoundId);
    if (compound) setOpenCompound(compound);
    onInitialCompoundHandled?.();
  }, [initialCompoundId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    api
      .listCompoundVotes()
      .then((d) => setVotes(d.votes))
      .catch((err) => setError(err.message));
  }, []);

  // Card-level summary (structure thumbnail, formula, how many curated
  // mechanisms, whether a hand-written profile exists) from the same cached
  // payload the detail view uses, so opening a compound costs no extra fetch.
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    loadCompoundData()
      .then(({ interactions, chem, profiles }) => {
        if (!live) return;
        const out = {};
        for (const c of COMPOUND_CATALOG) {
          const ch = chem.compounds[c.id];
          out[c.id] = {
            image: ch ? ch.image : null,
            formula: ch ? ch.formula : null,
            edges: (interactions[c.id] || []).length,
            human: (interactions[c.id] || []).filter((e) => e.evidenceTier === "human_rct" || e.evidenceTier === "human_observational").length,
            curated: !!(profiles.compounds || {})[c.id],
            unresolved: chem.unresolved[c.id] || null,
          };
        }
        setMeta(out);
      })
      .catch(() => {});
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, []);

  async function castVote(tag, category, value) {
    if (!user) return;
    const key = `${tag}:${category}`;
    if (busyKey) return;
    const current = (votes[tag] && votes[tag][category]) || emptyVote();
    const nextValue = current.myVote === value ? 0 : value;
    setBusyKey(key);
    setError("");
    try {
      const result = await api.voteCompound(tag, category, nextValue);
      setVotes((v) => ({
        ...v,
        [tag]: { ...(v[tag] || {}), [category]: { up: result.up, down: result.down, myVote: result.myVote } },
      }));
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setBusyKey(null);
    }
  }

  const groups = activeGroup === "all" ? COMPOUND_GROUPS : COMPOUND_GROUPS.filter((g) => g.id === activeGroup);

  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  const matches = (c) =>
    !q ||
    c.label.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
    c.desc.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
    c.id.includes(q.replace(/\s+/g, "-")) ||
    (c.aliases || []).some((alias) => alias.toLowerCase().includes(q));
  const shown = COMPOUND_CATALOG.filter(matches).sort((a, b) => {
    if (sortBy === "evidence") return (meta[b.id]?.human || 0) - (meta[a.id]?.human || 0) || (meta[b.id]?.edges || 0) - (meta[a.id]?.edges || 0) || a.label.localeCompare(b.label);
    if (sortBy === "coverage") return (meta[b.id]?.edges || 0) - (meta[a.id]?.edges || 0) || a.label.localeCompare(b.label);
    return a.label.localeCompare(b.label);
  });
  const requestNeedle = normalizeCompoundName(requestForm.name);
  const requestMatches = requestNeedle.length < 1 ? [] : COMPOUND_CATALOG.filter((c) =>
    [c.label, ...(c.aliases || [])].some((name) => normalizeCompoundName(name).includes(requestNeedle))
  ).slice(0, 10);
  const exactRequestMatch = requestNeedle && COMPOUND_CATALOG.find((c) =>
    [c.label, ...(c.aliases || [])].some((name) => normalizeCompoundName(name) === requestNeedle)
  );

  async function submitCompoundRequest(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (exactRequestMatch) {
      setRequestState(`${exactRequestMatch.label} is already in the catalog.`);
      return;
    }
    setRequestState("Submitting…");
    try {
      await api.submitCompound(requestForm);
      setRequestForm({ name: "", aliases: "", rationale: "", evidenceUrls: "", safetyNotes: "" });
      setRequestState("Submitted for admin review.");
    } catch (err) {
      setRequestState(err.message);
    }
  }

  return html`
    <div class="compounds-tab">
      ${error && html`<div class="post-modal__error">${error}</div>`}

      <div class="compounds-tab__search search">
        <svg class="search__icon" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2">
          <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8" />
          <line x1="21" y1="21" x2="16.65" y2="16.65" />
        </svg>
        <input
          class="search__input"
          type="search"
          placeholder="Search compounds — name, class, or what it does"
          value=${query}
          onInput=${(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
        />
      </div>

      <div class="topic-filter">
        <button
          class=${`pill pill--tag ${activeGroup === "all" ? "pill--active" : ""}`}
          onClick=${() => setActiveGroup("all")}
        >
          All
        </button>
        ${COMPOUND_GROUPS.map(
          (g) => html`
            <button
              key=${g.id}
              class=${`pill pill--tag ${activeGroup === g.id ? "pill--active" : ""}`}
              onClick=${() => setActiveGroup(g.id)}
            >
              ${g.label}
            </button>
          `
        )}
      </div>

      <div class="results-count">
        ${q ? `${shown.length} of ${COMPOUND_CATALOG.length} compounds` : `${COMPOUND_CATALOG.length} compounds`}
        <span class="compounds-tab__hint"> · click any compound for the full breakdown</span>
      </div>
      <div class="compounds-tab__tools">
        <span>Sort</span>
        <select class="authbox__input compounds-tab__sort" value=${sortBy} onChange=${(e) => setSortBy(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="name">Name</option>
          <option value="evidence">Human evidence</option>
          <option value="coverage">Mechanism coverage</option>
        </select>
        <span class="compounds-tab__legend"><b>Human</b> = human-linked mechanisms · <b>Preclinical</b> = animal/cell only</span>
      </div>

      <div class="compound-request">
        <button class="admin-accept" type="button" onClick=${() => setShowRequest((v) => !v)}>
          ${showRequest ? "Close request form" : "+ Add compound"}
        </button>
        ${showRequest && html`
          <form class="compound-request__form" onSubmit=${submitCompoundRequest}>
            <p class="stack-tab__hint-text">Suggest a missing compound. Requests are private and visible only to admins.</p>
            <div class="compound-request__lookup">
              <input class="authbox__input" required maxlength="100" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Start typing a compound name…" value=${requestForm.name}
                onInput=${(e) => { setRequestState(""); setRequestForm((f) => ({ ...f, name: e.target.value })); }} />
              ${requestMatches.length > 0 && html`<div class="compound-request__matches" role="listbox">
                <div class="compound-request__matches-head">Already in Pharmapedia</div>
                ${requestMatches.map((c) => html`<button type="button" key=${c.id} class="compound-request__match"
                  onClick=${() => { setOpenCompound(c); setShowRequest(false); }}>
                  <span><b>${c.label}</b>${c.aliases?.length ? html`<small>${c.aliases.join(" · ")}</small>` : null}</span>
                  <span>Open →</span>
                </button>`)}
              </div>`}
            </div>
            <input class="authbox__input" maxlength="300" placeholder="Aliases / brand names" value=${requestForm.aliases}
              onInput=${(e) => setRequestForm((f) => ({ ...f, aliases: e.target.value }))} />
            <textarea class="authbox__input" required maxlength="1500" placeholder="Why should it be added? Intended use or relevant threat…" value=${requestForm.rationale}
              onInput=${(e) => setRequestForm((f) => ({ ...f, rationale: e.target.value }))}></textarea>
            <textarea class="authbox__input" maxlength="2000" placeholder="PubMed links, PMIDs, DOIs, or trial links (one per line)" value=${requestForm.evidenceUrls}
              onInput=${(e) => setRequestForm((f) => ({ ...f, evidenceUrls: e.target.value }))}></textarea>
            <textarea class="authbox__input" maxlength="1500" placeholder="Known risks, interactions, contraindications, or uncertainty" value=${requestForm.safetyNotes}
              onInput=${(e) => setRequestForm((f) => ({ ...f, safetyNotes: e.target.value }))}></textarea>
            <button class="admin-accept" type="submit" disabled=${requestState === "Submitting…" || !!exactRequestMatch}>Submit request</button>
            ${requestState && html`<span class="compound-request__status">${requestState}</span>`}
          </form>
        `}
      </div>

      ${groups.map((g) => {
        const inGroup = shown.filter((c) => c.group === g.id);
        if (!inGroup.length) return null;
        return html`
          <div key=${g.id} class="compound-group">
            <h3 class="compound-group__title">${g.label}</h3>
            <div class="compounds-tab__grid">
              ${inGroup.map((c) => {
                const stats = meta[c.id];
                return html`
                  <button
                    key=${c.id}
                    class="compound-card"
                    onClick=${() => setOpenCompound(c)}
                    aria-label=${`Open ${c.label} details`}
                  >
                    <div class="compound-card__head">
                      ${stats && stats.image
                        ? html`<img
                            class="compound-card__thumb"
                            src=${`/src/data/${stats.image}`}
                            alt=""
                            loading="lazy"
                          />`
                        : html`<span class="compound-card__thumb compound-card__thumb--none"></span>`}
                      <div class="compound-card__title">${c.label}</div>
                    </div>
                    <p class="compound-card__desc">${c.desc}</p>
                    <div class="compound-card__foot">
                      ${stats && stats.formula ? html`<span class="compound-card__formula">${stats.formula}</span>` : null}
                      ${stats && stats.edges
                        ? html`<span>${stats.edges} mechanism${stats.edges === 1 ? "" : "s"}</span>`
                        : null}
                      ${stats && stats.human > 0
                        ? html`<span class="compound-card__evidence compound-card__evidence--human">${stats.human} human</span>`
                        : stats && stats.edges > 0
                          ? html`<span class="compound-card__evidence compound-card__evidence--preclinical">preclinical / inferred</span>`
                          : html`<span class="compound-card__evidence compound-card__evidence--limited">coverage pending</span>`}
                      ${stats && stats.unresolved ? html`<span title=${stats.unresolved}>multi-component / biologic</span>` : null}
                      ${stats && stats.curated ? html`<span class="compound-card__curated">full profile</span>` : null}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                `;
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      })}

      ${openCompound &&
      html`
        <${CompoundDetail}
          compound=${openCompound}
          user=${user}
          votes=${votes}
          onVote=${castVote}
          busyKey=${busyKey}
          voteCategories=${VOTE_CATEGORIES}
          onClose=${() => setOpenCompound(null)}
        />
      `}
    </div>
  `;
}
